  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体依赖对通用大语言模型的专门提示工程，虽然在简单操作中表现尚可，但难以应对真实世界应用中典型的复杂多步工作流。因此需要一种专门针对电子表格任务训练的智能体框架。

Method: Spreadsheet-RL包含三个核心组件：(1) 自动化流水线，从在线论坛规模化收集配对的起始-目标电子表格；(2) Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集，涵盖金融、供应链管理等领域的评估任务；(3) Spreadsheet Gym环境，通过Python沙箱暴露全面的Excel功能，并配备精细的工具集和工具路由规则，支持多轮强化学习训练。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench上，Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%；在Domain-Spreadsheet数据集上，Pass@1从8.4%提升至17.2%。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL在通用和领域特定的电子表格任务上均显著提升了AI智能体的性能，展现出强大的泛化能力和实际应用潜力，为推进基于LLM的数据界面交互提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [110] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtelierEval是首个统一量化提示能力的基准，覆盖360个专家设计任务，通过AtelierJudge评估器实现接近人类水平的评估，揭示模仿优于规划，倡导图像增强的提示发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I基准仅评估模型本身，忽略了上游提示器（人类或MLLM）的提示能力，缺乏统一的量化标准。

Method: 构建AtelierEval基准（360个专家任务，基于认知视角分三类，双界面支持人类和MLLM），提出AtelierJudge评估器（基于技能、记忆增强的智能体评估器，生成主客观评分）。

Result: AtelierJudge与人类专家的Spearman相关系数达0.79；在8个MLLM和48个人类用户、4个T2I后端上验证了基准的有效性；揭示模仿策略优于规划策略。

Conclusion: AtelierEval是可靠的诊断工具，揭示了当前提示器的不足，并建议未来朝图像增强的提示方向发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [111] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab 是一个实验室原生的自主研究平台，通过可定制角色、协作工作流、实时监控和回滚控制等功能，将自主研究从隐藏的提示到论文流水线转变为交互式 AI 实验室，并引入 Claw-Code Harness 实现本地代码库与实验的直接连接，在五项 AI 研究案例中优于基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统通常是单一智能体或固定串行工作流的"黑箱"流水线，缺乏可交互性、可控性和可靠性，导致用户难以监控、调试和复现实验，且容易产生不完整运行和错误报告等问题。

Method: 1) 用户只需一个提示即可实例化完整研究团队，支持自定义角色和协作工作流；2) 提供统一仪表盘，支持实时监控、制品检查、回滚和恢复控制；3) 支持探索、多智能体讨论和复现三种研究模式；4) 核心组件 Claw-Code Harness 连接本地代码库、数据集和检查点，将执行制品反馈回研究循环。

Result: 在五项 AI 研究案例的内部评估中，以 AutoResearchClaw 为基线，Claw AI Lab 在想法新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量三个维度上均获得 AI 专家评审的一致偏好。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab 是向新的自主研究范式迈出的早期一步，即实现可用、可交互且关注可靠性的自主科学基础设施。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [112] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在具有超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险的预测任务（如金融、流行病学）上存在逆缩放现象：越强大的模型产生的分布预测越差。该问题集中在分布上尾，源于模型对增长的激进外推。单阈值指标会掩盖这一缺陷，建议使用连续无界精度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测基准通常使用单阈值指标评估模型能力，忽略了分布预测中尾部风险的评估。本文旨在揭示在具有超线性增长和体制转换风险的结构化预测任务中，更强大的LLM反而表现更差这一反直觉现象。

Method: 构造无污染模拟基准FBSim，包含SIR流行病预测及其线性对照；在COVID-19、麻疹、住房市场、恶性通胀等真实数据上复现；进行分位数分解定位误差来源；在Llama-3.1系列内部分离模型规模和后训练的影响；对比单阈值与尾部包含性评估指标的效果。

Result: 逆缩放现象集中在上尾分位数，更强大模型将上尾上移以追踪激进的外推增长；模型规模和后训练均独立贡献该效应；领域知识无法可靠挽救校准；单阈值指标完全翻转能力-准确度关系符号，掩盖逆缩放。

Conclusion: LLM预测评估应使用连续无界精度指标补充二值阈值指标，以避免尾部风险的系统性误判。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [113] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUSP基准框架，系统评估AI系统在科学预测方面的能力。通过对4,760个科学事件的评估发现，当前前沿模型虽然在识别可行研究方向上有一定能力，但在预测科学进展是否实现、何时实现方面存在系统性缺陷，且表现出过度自信和响应偏差。模型性能在领域间差异显著，AI领域的时间预测优于生物学、化学和物理学。性能对训练数据截止前后的事件不敏感，说明局限不能仅归因于知识暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI能否预测科学进展这一核心问题，建立时间锚定的评估框架来系统检验AI在科学预测方面的能力边界。

Method: 构建CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准，包含4,760个科学事件，从可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计和时间预测四个维度评估AI系统的科学预测能力。

Result: 1) 模型能识别可行研究方向但无法可靠预测进展是否实现；2) 时间预测系统性偏差；3) 跨领域性能高度异质，AI领域优于生物/化学/物理；4) 训练数据截止前后性能无显著差异；5) 先验知识提升性能但无法赶上全信息设置；6) 模型表现出系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学预测工具存在根本性局限：先验知识无法转化为可靠预测能力，模型更多受益于事后信息而非前瞻性预测。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22707)
*Stephanie Rosenthal,Shamsi Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对一家大型科技公司24名产品人员的访谈，本研究探讨了AI如何改变正式角色职责、团队协作以及非正式文化实践（如职业指导），指出AI带来的变化既有积极方面（如协作更顺畅），也存在削弱职业成长机会（如反馈、领导力和 mentorship）的风险，并提出了让隐形工作显性化及维护健康公司文化的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在AI优先的公司中，工作流程如何发生变化，特别是AI如何影响个体的工作、产品团队内的协作以及专业互动。

Method: 对一家大型科技公司的24名产品聚焦人员进行半结构化访谈，收集关于AI如何影响其个人工作、团队协作和职业互动的定性数据。

Result: AI不仅改变正式的角色职责和协作模式，还改变了非正式文化实践（如职业指导）；部分变化是积极的（如同级协作更顺畅），但另一些变化则对职业成长机会（如接收反馈、领导力培养和 mentorship）构成风险。

Conclusion: 提出了AI公司让隐形工作显性化的步骤，以及个人和领导者如何在AI转型中支持同事，同时维护鼓励多元思维、协作和非正式互动的健康公司文化。

Abstract: An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.

</details>


### [115] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型作为自动评估者时，对话历史极性（正面或负面）对后续判断的累积偏差效应（AMEL）。通过75,898次API调用测试11个模型，发现模型会偏向对话历史的主流极性，尤其在模型本身不确定时影响更大。负面历史比正面历史造成的偏差多1.62倍。偏差不随上下文长度增长，但增大模型规模可缓解。最优修复方案是每项评估使用新的上下文，若必须批量处理则需平衡历史极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常被用作自动评估工具（如代码审查、内容审核、评分），且通常在同一对话中处理多个项目。研究者质疑先前的对话历史极性是否会干扰后续判断，即是否存在累积的上下文偏差效应。

Method: 在75,898次API调用中，对11个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google和四个开源模型）进行测试。将相同的测试项目分别放在隔离环境（无历史）和填充了正面或负面评估的历史之后呈现，比较模型判断的差异。进一步通过三个后续实验探究机制：token概率分布变化、负性不对称的分解、以及偏见在对话历史中的位置效应。

Result: 模型会向对话历史的主流极性偏移（d = -0.17, p < 10^-46）；该效应集中在模型基线不确定的项目上（高熵项目d = -0.34 vs 确定性项目d = -0.15）；偏差不随上下文长度增长（5轮与50轮效果相同）；负面历史造成的偏差是正面的1.62倍；模型规模增大可缓解但无法消除（如Haiku -0.22到Opus -0.17）。

Conclusion: 对话历史极性会系统性偏差LLM作为评估者的判断，最优解决方案是每项评估使用全新上下文；若必须批量处理，则需平衡历史中的正面和负面评价以减轻偏差。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [116] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为参数化模块逻辑程序的形式化方法，用于增强一阶回答集编程（ASP）的模块化能力。该方法允许定义带参数和内涵性声明的子程序，能够捕捉clingo-programs中集体控制特性的语义，为模块化ASP提供了理论基础，并建立了与传统非模块化ASP的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶回答集编程（ASP）缺乏有效的模块化机制，难以支持大型复杂程序的模块化开发和复用。作者希望通过引入参数化模块逻辑程序来解决这一问题，使得子程序可以带参数和内涵性声明，从而增强ASP的结构化能力和可维护性。

Method: 提出参数化模块逻辑程序形式化方法，允许子程序定义参数和内涵性声明；以clingo-programs的集体控制（collective control）特性作为案例，展示该形式化如何捕捉其语义；建立模块化ASP的理论基础，并证明其与传统非模块化ASP的关联。

Result: 成功定义了参数化模块逻辑程序的形式化语义，证明了其能够捕捉clingo-programs中集体控制特性的语义，并建立了模块化ASP与传统非模块化ASP之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 参数化模块逻辑程序为一阶ASP提供了坚实的模块化理论基础，能够有效支持子程序的结构化定义和实例化，为未来ASP语言的实际模块化扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22732)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究声学情感识别模型能否替代TRUST多智能体LLM流水线中的Pathos维度进行政治演讲分析。以Bundestag演讲为案例，对比三种模态的分析方法，发现Gemini Valence与TRUST-Pathos显著相关(rho=+0.664)，而声学模型无效(rho=+0.097)。进一步通过对EMO-DB的质量评估，揭示标准SER基准语料库存在表演性语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容等问题。结果表明LLM多模态分析远优于纯声学模型，但声学特征对低层唤醒度估计仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政治演讲情感分析多依赖声学情感识别模型，但这类模型能否有效捕捉政治语境下的Pathos（情感说服）维度尚不清楚。作者希望验证LLM多模态分析是否比纯声学模型更适合这一任务。

Method: 以Felix Banaszak在Bundestag的51个片段（245秒）演讲为案例，比较三种分析模态：(1) emotion2vec_plus_large声学SER模型（通过Russell Circumplex投影得到连续Arousal和Valence）；(2) Gemini 2.5 Flash对音频+转录本的开放式上下文感知分析；(3) TRUST-Pathos来自三智能体LLM监督集成得分。使用Spearman秩相关评估，并在EMO-DB上进行系统性质量评估。

Result: Gemini Valence与TRUST-Pathos显著相关(rho=+0.664, p<0.001)，而emotion2vec Valence不相关(rho=+0.097, p=0.499)。EMO-DB评估揭示标准SER基准存在表演性语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容问题。

Conclusion: LLM多模态分析在捕捉语义定义的政治情感方面显著优于纯声学模型，但声学特征对低层Arousal估计仍有参考价值。未来将扩展到包含面部表情和注视的视频分析。

Abstract: We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.

</details>


### [118] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了利用LLM生成Lean形式化证明来解决开放数学问题的能力。最强智能体以数百美元的成本自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，以及492个OEIS猜想中的44个，目前正被部署于组合学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究。基础智能体通过交替LLM生成与Lean验证复制了Erdős成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理方面日益强大，但其不可靠性限制了在数学研究中的应用。一种缓解方案是使用LLM生成形式化证明（如Lean语言），但该方法解决开放问题的能力尚未得到大规模评估。

Method: 1) 进行首个大规模评估，测试LLM辅助形式化证明搜索方法解决开放数学问题的能力。2) 设计最强智能体（agent）和基础智能体两种架构，前者以较低成本自主解决多个开放问题，后者交替使用LLM生成证明与Lean验证。

Result: 最强智能体以数百美元/问题的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个。基础智能体复现了Erdős问题的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: 研究表明AI辅助形式化证明搜索在解决开放数学问题方面具有强大能力，并揭示了实现这一能力的智能体设计范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [119] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，用于保护基于KV缓存的多智能体LLM系统中的潜在通信安全，通过表示级变换防止敏感信息泄露，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统中，中间通信（尤其是通过Transformer KV缓存的潜在通信）在提高效率的同时，也编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成了一个不透明渠道，敏感内容可能在不经显式文本披露的情况下跨智能体传播，存在安全隐患。

Method: LCGuard将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存工件跨智能体传输前学习表示级变换。通过对抗性训练框架： adversary学习重建敏感输入，LCGuard学习保留任务相关语义同时减少可重建信息的变换。

Result: 在多个模型家族和多智能体基准上的实验表明，LCGuard持续降低了基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率，同时与标准KV共享基线相比保持了有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效平衡了KV缓存间通信的效率与隐私保护需求，为多智能体系统中的潜在通信安全提供了可行的防护方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过分离擦除门和写入门，改进了 Delta 规则线性注意力模型，在长上下文检索任务上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Delta 规则线性注意力模型（如 Gated DeltaNet 和 KDA）使用单一标量门控同时控制擦除旧内容和写入新内容，导致记忆编辑能力受限。本文旨在解耦这两个操作，实现更精确的压缩记忆管理。

Method: 提出 Gated DeltaNet-2，引入通道级擦除门 b_t 和通道级写入门 w_t 替代单一标量门控；推导了快速权重更新视角、带有通道级衰减的块状 WY 算法，以及支持高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数、100B FineWeb-Edu 训练下，Gated DeltaNet-2 在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上全面超越 Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA 和 Mamba-3，尤其在长上下文 RULER 多键检索任务上优势显著。

Conclusion: 分离擦除与写入门控是提升 Delta 规则线性注意力长上下文能力的关键，Gated DeltaNet-2 在循环和混合设置下均表现强劲，代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [121] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSS 是一个在源代码层面实现生产环境智能体系统自我演化的框架。它通过自动收集失败证据、多阶段确定性代码修改、回放验证和容器热替换，在无需人工干预的情况下，将 OpenClaw 上四任务平均评分从 0.25 提升至 0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体系统部署后无法从用户交互中学习，重复性失败只能等待人工更新修复。虽有自演化智能体出现，但所有演化都局限于文本可变构件（技能文件、提示配置、记忆模式、工作流图等），而路由、钩子排序、状态不变量、调度等核心逻辑固化在代码中，导致一类结构性失败在文本层面无法触及。

Method: MOSS 在源代码层面进行自我改写：每次演化锚定于自动整理的批量生产失败证据，经过确定性多阶段流水线处理；代码修改委托给可插拔的外部编码智能体 CLI，MOSS 保留阶段排序和裁决；候选版本通过在临时试验工作器中回放失败批次进行验证，验证通过后经用户同意、通过健康探针回滚机制的就地容器热替换进行部署。

Result: 在 OpenClaw 上，MOSS 在单次演化循环中无需人工干预即将四任务平均评分从 0.25 提升至 0.61。

Conclusion: 源代码级自适应是一种更通用的演化媒介：图灵完备、严格超集于所有文本可变作用域、确定性生效而非依赖基座模型合规、且不会因长上下文漂移而退化。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于可预测的nonce重用导致的ECDSA私钥恢复漏洞，攻击者可通过线性代数方法从签名中提取私钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易依赖ECDSA签名安全，而nonce生成是核心；Polygon MEV生态中searcher为满足密封投标拍卖的亚秒级响应而使用可预测nonce模式，导致严重安全隐患。

Method: 通过分析链上数据识别nonce重用模式，构建线性方程组攻击模型（涵盖跨钱包nonce碰撞场景），使用基础代数方法恢复私钥。

Result: 发现了可被利用的签名模式，证明了协议导致的延迟压力会引发生产系统中的灾难性密码学失败，单个实现错误可同时危及多个账户。

Conclusion: 协议设计中的延迟约束会间接诱发密码学实现缺陷，需在MEV生态中强制引入安全的nonce生成机制。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [123] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY数据集，整合了跨编译器、跨优化等级、跨历史版本的73,610个二进制文件及CVE标签，并通过三项分析验证其在二进制漏洞分析中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库要么只包含跨编译器的静态构建（缺乏时间维度），要么只提供单一构建的CVE标签，缺乏同时包含跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签的可查询结构。

Method: 收集73,610个二进制文件，涵盖248个开源项目，使用GCC/Clang/MSVC在Linux/Windows上以多种优化等级编译，包含多年历史构建。每个二进制文件与源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和漏洞函数关联，存入数据库。通过三项分析验证：(1)三阶段LLM基准测试；(2)MalConv、jTrans和TLSH嵌入空间的聚类比较；(3)贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性贡献因素。

Result: 构建了包含73,610个二进制文件、248个项目、多编译器多平台多优化等级带CVE标签的大型数据集，并提供了三种分析方法的实验结果。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY填补了现有二进制语料库在跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合方面的空白，为二进制漏洞分析和LLM推理研究提供了统一框架和数据基础。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [124] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个多LLM协作的推理驱动框架，通过将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，高效生成越狱提示，以更高成功率、更低计算成本攻击对齐的LLM，且生成的提示极少被检测为有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在现实世界中广泛部署，但仍然容易受到越狱攻击（基于提示的绕过安全过滤器）。现有方法的效率和效果有限，需要更高效、更有效的攻击框架来发现未知漏洞，从而主动强化模型安全性。

Method: 提出THREAT框架：一个推理驱动的多LLM协作系统，在迭代搜索循环中协调多个LLM以发现文本越狱提示。将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，并提供高效求解方案，降低运行时间并提高攻击效果。

Result: THREAT在多种数据集和模型架构上实现了更高的攻击成功率（ASR）和更低的计算成本。生成的提示仅有不到1%被标记为有害，而对应的未修改提示约有50%被拒绝。

Conclusion: 揭示了对齐LLM中此前未检测到的漏洞，THREAT可作为主动强化基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [125] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2是一个基于多智能体系统的自动化漏洞检测框架，结合OSS-Fuzz模糊测试、控制流抽象（Suspicious Point）、逻辑驱动的层次化函数分析和MCP上下文工程，在AIxCC 2025决赛数据集上达到90%检测率，并在真实部署中发现29个零日漏洞（2个已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based漏洞检测存在三大挑战：高误报率且缺乏可复现验证、漏洞定位粒度（函数级/行级）不优化、难以推理具有复杂跨函数依赖和触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动漏洞分析确保模糊测试可复现；(2) 提出Suspicious Point控制流抽象实现最优粒度的精确漏洞定位；(3) 逻辑驱动的层次化函数分析与双层模糊测试增强资源约束下的函数覆盖率；(4) 基于MCP的静态与动态分析工具结合上下文工程增强复杂漏洞推理。

Result: AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上检测率90%（36/40）；真实部署中发现29个零日漏洞，覆盖12个开源项目，2个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过多智能体协作与模糊测试驱动的可复现验证，有效解决了LLM在漏洞检测中的高误报率和复杂推理难题，验证了自动化漏洞发现在实际场景中的可行性与效果。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [126] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Arrow原生DataFrame引擎Polars在Intel SGX2安全飞地中的性能表现，发现端到端安全开销约为1.5倍，但表加载开销随数据集增大而显著上升（2.27倍至4.07倍），而纯查询开销则略有下降；惰性执行比急切执行快约2.25倍，且后者在41GB以上数据集会因内存不足而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE机密分析研究多集中在SQL数据库引擎或较早期的SGX一代上，缺乏对现代Arrow原生引擎（如Polars）在SGX2中表现的评估，尤其是数据加载与查询计算开销的分离分析以及API执行模式（惰性与急切）的对比研究。

Method: 在TPC-H SF30基准测试上，通过Gramine将Polars部署于Intel SGX2飞地，使用Azure Blob Storage进行数据存储，测量标准TPC-H power score及纯查询变体指标，在四种数据集宽度配置（约22-73GB）下分别分析端到端开销、表加载开销和查询开销，并对比惰性与急切API的执行性能。

Result: 端到端安全开销稳定在1.49-1.56倍；查询开销从1.51-1.52倍降至1.43-1.44倍；表加载开销从2.27倍升至4.07倍；len130配置下单查询中位数SGX减速为1.45倍，最大达2.57倍；惰性执行整体比急切执行快2.25-2.27倍，急切执行在41GB以上出现OOM错误。

Conclusion: SGX2能够支持Arrow原生分析处理，安全开销与DuckDB-SGX2研究处于同一量级；但数据加载路径的开销放大和API层面的优化是决定端到端性能的首要因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [127] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，明确了路径长度与循环结构的特征，揭示了图在二元与三元分量作用下的强正则性，为密码学应用中的安全性分析提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力学复杂性及其在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式在2和3的幂次模下的新性质，将环中元素视为顶点、多项式映射关系视为有向边，对函数图中的路径长度和循环结构进行显式刻画。

Result: 表明尽管二元和三元分量引入了复杂性，但函数图展现出强正则性，包括给定长度的循环数量恒定，以及随 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果扩展了先前在素幂环上的工作，为数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持了其密码学应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [128] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE方法，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则以绕过沙箱逃逸检测，在334个真实恶意软件样本上达到79%的绕过成功率，并通过迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例，相比现有平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸检测依赖人工逆向分析每个逃逸机制来编写绕过规则，这种方法无法应对快速演变的逃逸技术，亟需自动化方案。

Method: ABLE利用大语言模型分析恶意软件因潜在逃逸行为被终止的执行轨迹，采用多种推理策略生成针对性YARA绕过规则，并通过自动清洗管道和反馈驱动的迭代优化解决语法错误并提升规则效果。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本上，ABLE在四种开源LLM上达到79%的绕过成功率，其中迭代优化贡献29.5%；相比现有平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类，并暴露了先前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: ABLE证明了大语言模型在自动化沙箱逃逸规则生成方面的有效性，显著提升了恶意软件分析的覆盖率和效率。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [129] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz提出四项原则（P1-P4）作为harness正确性的首个源级定义，并通过LLM智能体的"生成-检查-修复"循环实现自动化harness生成，在23个开源项目中发现29个已确认/修复的漏洞（含3个CVE），误报率仅4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试工具自动化生成harness时，无法在源码层面检测逻辑错误、API误用和生命周期违规问题。随着LLM驱动的harness生成规模化，不受控的质量将把规模变为负担。

Method: 提出四项原则框架（P1逻辑正确性、P2 API协议合规、P3安全边界尊重、P4入口点充分性）及数学规范与可实现检查，并构建基于LLM的自主智能体，通过"生成-检查-修复"循环确保harness满足P1-P4。

Result: 在23个开源项目（C/C++、Java、JavaScript）中提交42个bug报告，29个已修复/确认（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（4.8%误报率）。P1/P2检查自动拦截58个harness导致的崩溃。审计586个生产环境harness发现53个违规（45确认，35已修复）。

Conclusion: QuartetFuzz通过系统化的正确性保障机制，有效提升了自动化harness生成的质量，在漏洞发现和harness审计上均取得显著成果，为LLM驱动的模糊测试自动化提供了可靠的质量控制方案。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [130] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出baseSPIDER和SPIDER两种私密信息检索方案。baseSPIDER在单服务器有状态客户端场景下，实现了与现有方案相同的渐进最优通信复杂度，但改善了常数因子，且设计更简洁。SPIDER通过将baseSPIDER简单转换，成为首个无需服务器特殊配合、可在默认数据库接口上运行的单服务器PIR方案，消除了部署障碍。该转换方法还可推广至其他三种PIR方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器PIR方案在通信复杂度上虽达到渐进最优，但常数因子较大，且通常需要服务器提供特殊API或协议特定交互，部署门槛高。本文旨在通过更简洁的设计降低常数因子，并消除对服务器端特殊配合的需求，提升PIR方案的实际可用性。

Method: baseSPIDER采用单服务器有状态客户端模型，客户端通过预处理存储提示信息，实现了优于现有方案的常数因子。SPIDER通过一个简单变换将baseSPIDER适配到默认数据库接口场景，无需服务器任何特殊合作或专用API。该变换方法也被推广应用于其他三种近期的PIR方案。

Result: baseSPIDER在通信复杂度上匹配现有最优方案的渐进界并改善常数因子，尤其适用于大条目数据库。SPIDER是首个可在默认服务器接口上运行的单服务器PIR方案，无需服务器端任何特殊配置。与经相同变换修改后的其他方案相比，SPIDER设计更简单，但客户端计算开销更高。

Conclusion: baseSPIDER和SPIDER分别在有状态客户端和默认服务器两种场景下提供了更实用、更易部署的PIR方案。SPIDER及其变换方法为PIR技术的实际落地提供了新路径，降低了系统集成门槛。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [131] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了一种针对LLM智能体注入检测器的系统性盲点——领域伪装注入（domain camouflaged injection），即当注入载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时，标准检测器的检测率骤降（如从93.8%降至9.7%）。作者定义了伪装检测差距（CDG），在多领域、多模型上验证了其显著性和普遍性，并发现生产级安全分类器Llama Guard 3完全无法检测伪装载荷。多智能体辩论架构在小模型上会放大静态注入攻击，而针对性的检测器增强仅能部分缓解，表明该漏洞对弱模型而言是架构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体注入检测器基于静态、模板化的载荷进行校准，这些载荷明确宣称为覆写指令。作者识别出一个系统性盲点：当注入载荷通过模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构进行伪装时，标准检测器会完全失效。

Method: 1) 形式化定义伪装检测差距（CDG），即静态载荷与伪装载荷之间的注入检测率差异。2) 在45个任务、3个领域、2个模型家族（Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash）上进行实验评估。3) 评估生产级安全分类器Llama Guard 3。4) 分析多智能体辩论架构对注入攻击的影响。5) 测试针对性检测器增强的缓解效果。

Result: 静态检测率从93.8%骤降至伪装下的9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B），从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。CDG在统计上显著（p < 0.001），零反向不一致对。Llama Guard 3完全检测不到伪装载荷（IDR=0.000）。多智能体辩论在小模型上放大静态注入攻击达9.9倍，强模型则表现集体抵抗。针对性增强仅部分缓解（Llama提升10.2%，Gemini提升78.7%）。

Conclusion: 领域伪装注入是LLM智能体安全的一个系统性、架构性漏洞，对弱模型尤为严重。现有检测器无论是少样本检测器还是专用安全分类器均存在此盲点，且简单的检测器增强无法完全修复。作者开源了框架、任务库和载荷生成器以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [132] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个利用大语言模型从自然语言安全查询自动生成可执行查询代码的系统，在133个查询中比人工脚本错误率降低3倍以上，尤其擅长多行时间关联查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志源使用各自的半结构化格式，构建解析器成本高，而直接查询原始日志需要分析师了解各源的消息变体且无法表达多行时间查询，需要弥合结构化日志查询的表达性与原始日志直接查询的即时性之间的差距。

Method: 利用大语言模型，结合自动提取的轻量级日志格式上下文，仅需一次LLM调用即可生成可执行查询代码，随后进行确定性执行。

Result: 在5种日志类型的133个安全查询上，Sieve在复杂时间查询和跨事件查询中相比人工分析师脚本错误率降低3倍以上，在多行关联任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达性与直接处理原始日志的即时性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [133] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一种针对生成式API服务的防御框架，通过在合成图像中注入频率感知的、不可感知的扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时降低被窃取用于训练替代模型的效用，实现了有效性-保真度-效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭权重的生成式服务通过API部署时面临模型窃取威胁：攻击者可反复查询API收集合成图像数据，用于训练私有替代模型实现未经授权的知识蒸馏和能力复制。现有防御需要一种能保持视觉保真度、提供扰动幅度显式控制、并能高效扩展到大规模输出发布的实用方案。

Method: WaveGuard采用单次前向、基于生成器的保护框架。其核心是频率感知的扰动生成器，在用户指定的扰动预算下，向合成图像注入结构化、不可感知的扰动，在保持良性观看者感知效用同时降低受保护图像作为未授权学生模型训练数据的效用。

Result: 在WikiArt相关的合成输出蒸馏设置下的大规模实验表明，WaveGuard实现了有效性-保真度-效率三者之间的有利权衡，具有显式的不可感知性控制和保护效率的显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard为生成式API服务提供了一种实用的模型窃取防御方案，通过频率感知扰动在视觉质量与防御效果之间取得平衡，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [134] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流的物理不可克隆函数，用于超低功耗医疗物联网设备身份认证。采用伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路将皮安级泄漏变化转为数字响应，在65nm CMOS工艺下实现0.9999998熵值、0.5001平均片间汉明距离、96.04nW/bit功耗，在0.9-1.3V和0-100°C范围内可靠工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网设备面临物理篡改和伪造威胁，现有PUF（如基于存储器、环形振荡器、仲裁器的方案）因额外电路开销、功耗高或稳定性差，不适用于超低功耗植入式和可穿戴医疗设备。

Method: 利用标准CMOS工艺中工艺波动导致的量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流作为熵源，设计带伪电阻I-V前端的差分读出电路，将皮安级泄漏电流差转换为数字响应；无需外部激励或稳定电路，静态偏置即可工作。

Result: 65nm CMOS仿真显示：熵值0.9999998，平均片间汉明距离0.5001，平均功耗96.04nW/bit（19.21fJ/bit），在0.9-1.3V和0-100°C范围内平均误码率低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF以极低功耗和面积开销实现了高安全性、高可靠性的硬件认证方案，特别适用于典型植入式医疗设备的工作条件（1.0-1.3V，10-70°C）。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [135] [Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122)
*Yutong Liu,Chenyi Wang,Ming F. Li,Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustFlip攻击，利用协同感知系统中一致性检测防御机制的漏洞，通过部署物理对抗物体使良性车辆间产生不一致观测，导致防御系统错误降低目标车辆的信任分数，最终将其排除协作。作者还提出TrustReflect作为初步缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知防御系统通过跨车辆不一致性检测和信任估计来抵御数据篡改攻击，但这些防御机制本身存在安全隐患，可能被利用来攻击良性车辆。

Method: TrustFlip攻击通过部署物理对抗物体（真实存在但易引发不一致观测的物体），使良性车辆间产生观测差异，防御系统将这些不一致归因于目标车辆，导致其信任分数下降并被排除协作。TrustReflect缓解机制通过自反射标记争议区域为不确定，将其排除出信任评估。

Result: 在多种协同感知架构和防御机制上评估，TrustFlip在高达87.7%的场景中成功将目标良性车辆排除协作，平均精度（AP）下降最多13%。TrustReflect将攻击成功率降低35-100%。

Conclusion: 揭示了协同感知防御机制的反向攻击面，表明现有一致性检测防御存在根本性缺陷，需要重新审视信任评估机制的设计。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) enables connected and autonomous vehicles to share sensor data and jointly reason about their environment. To defend against adversaries that fabricate or manipulate shared data, existing systems employ cross-vehicle inconsistency detection and trust estimation, penalizing vehicles whose observations conflict with the majority. In this work, we show that these defenses themselves introduce a new attack surface. We present TrustFlip, a novel attack that weaponizes consistency-based defenses to poison the trust assigned to benign vehicles. Instead of injecting false data into the collaboration pipeline, it deploys physical adversarial objects that are genuine but induce inconsistent observations among benign vehicles. The resulting inconsistencies are misattributed by the defense to the targeted vehicle, causing its trust score to degrade and eventually leading to its downweighting or exclusion from collaboration. Consequently, the system loses reliable sensing contributors, degrading perception capability and potentially inducing safety-critical failures. We evaluate TrustFlip across multiple collaborative perception architectures and defense mechanisms. Our results show that state-of-the-art defenses can be significantly affected: the attack removes the targeted benign vehicle from collaboration in up to 87.7% of scenarios and drops Average Precision (AP) by up to 13%. As an initial mitigation, we introduce TrustReflect, a lightweight self-reflection mechanism that marks disputed regions as uncertain and excludes them from trust evaluation, reducing the attack success rate by 35-100%.

</details>


### [136] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种Pareto感知的触发式主动学习框架，通过自适应窗口评分偏移触发和混合采集规则，在低样本率安全运营场景中显著降低误报负担（AIT-ADS降低43%，BOTSv1降低21%），同时将分析师查询次数减少3.8x和5.2x，优于冻结基线和周期性均匀随机更新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低阳性样本率的数据流中，即使很低的假阳性率也会产生大量调查负担，而聚合F1分数会掩盖分析师的真实工作负荷。现有方法未能有效权衡检测性能与运营负担。

Method: PACT包含三个核心组件：(1) 已部署的冻结XGBoost-Focal筛查器作为基础模型；(2) 自适应窗口评分偏移触发器，用于决定何时触发模型更新；(3) 混合采集规则，结合阈值相对不确定性与高分数采样的主动学习策略。通过Pareto感知的触发式主动学习，在保持可接受召回率的同时最小化假阳性负担。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个公开低样本率基准数据集上，PACT在所有自适应方法中达到最低的良性归一化假阳性负担：相比冻结基线分别降低43%和21%；相比周期性均匀随机更新，分析师查询次数减少3.8x和5.2x。消融实验显示采集策略对性能贡献超过触发时机，但代价是正样本窗口召回率下降约10个百分点；纯阈值基线虽FP更低，但BOTSv1召回率暴跌55个百分点。

Conclusion: PACT通过Pareto最优权衡实现了在低样本率安全告警场景中检测性能与运营负担之间的有效平衡。纯FP最小化策略会以不可接受的召回率下降为代价，表明在真实安全运营场景中，必须考虑多目标优化而非单一指标优化。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [137] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对远程MCP服务器的身份验证安全进行了大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器无认证暴露工具，且OAuth部署存在三类MCP特定特征导致新攻击面。对119台OAuth服务器检测发现每台至少存在一个漏洞，共识别325个漏洞，其中96.6%受动态客户端注册漏洞影响，已获得9个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议作为LLM与外部服务连接的通用接口正在兴起，远程部署中MCP客户端与服务器之间的身份验证边界至关重要但尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 发现7,973个实时远程MCP服务器；通过被动流量检测与主动动态探测相结合的半自动化检测框架，对119个可测试的OAuth MCP服务器进行大规模评估。

Result: 40.55%的服务器无认证暴露工具；OAuth部署呈现开放客户端环境、动态客户端注册和委托授权三类特征；每台服务器至少存在一个漏洞，共325个漏洞，动态客户端注册漏洞影响96.6%的测试服务器；已获得9个CVE编号。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统存在普遍的身份验证弱点，迫切需要加强基于OAuth的远程部署安全性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [138] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种后门防御在时间序列预测(TSF)中的失效原因，发现数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释、任务形式转换导致训练损失退化两大问题，并提出TimeGuard防御方法，采用通道级池训练、时间感知初始化池和距离正则化损失选择机制，在多个数据集和架构上显著提升鲁棒性（MAE_P提升1.96倍），同时保证干净性能损失在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测(TSF)在后门攻击下存在脆弱性，但针对TSF的后门防御研究严重不足，主要面临数据纠缠和任务形式转换两大挑战。

Method: 提出TimeGuard方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 通道级池训练(channel-wise pool training)作为核心范式，缓解信号稀释；(2) 时间感知初始化准则(time-aware criteria)构建高置信度初始池；(3) 距离正则化损失选择(distance-regularized loss selection)在训练中逐步扩展可靠池，缓解损失退化问题。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard相比最优基线将MAE_P提升1.96倍，同时干净性能损失(MAE_C)控制在5%以内。

Conclusion: 时间序列预测的后门防御需要针对性设计，传统的样本过滤和触发器合成防御因信号稀释和损失退化而失效，TimeGuard通过通道级池训练和正则化选择有效解决了这两大问题。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [139] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于少量已知良客户作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度降维聚类、伪梯度生成和重聚类三阶段，在动态投毒场景下有效识别并过滤恶意梯度，同时保留尽可能多的良性梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数，难以适应动态变化的投毒策略，且因客户端数据异构性导致良性梯度丢失严重。

Method: 1) 基于密度的低维梯度聚类，将梯度投影到两个最大发散维度上进行密度聚类；2) 增强聚类低维梯度生成器，学习生成位于良性聚类边界的伪梯度以连接稀疏良性离群点；3) 低维梯度重聚类，将伪梯度与真实梯度共同聚类以恢复被误分类为噪声点的良性梯度。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下展现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效应对恶意客户端动态变化的投毒策略，在未知且可变的恶意客户端数量下仍能准确识别恶意梯度并保留更多良性梯度参与聚合。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [140] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS——一个开源的操作技术（OT）渗透测试与安全分析平台，填补了OT领域缺乏类似Kali Linux这类开源安全工具的空白，并分享了其架构、构建和发布经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全领域已有Kali Linux等成熟的开源安全平台，但OT（操作技术）领域缺乏类似的开源平台，而OT支撑着工业控制系统，其安全分析需求迫切。

Method: 作者基于实践经验，架构、构建并发布了一个名为LINICS的开源OT渗透测试与安全分析平台。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS这一开源平台，为OT安全从业者提供了专用的渗透测试和安全分析工具。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT领域开源安全平台的空白，为工业控制系统的安全分析提供了可用的工具集。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


### [141] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep是一个四无人机编队仿真框架，通过空间分离的专用采集器（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器泄露、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄露）和汇聚无人机的相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）及二阶掩码抵消，在0.25-1.5 m距离实现嵌入式微控制器电磁侧信道分析，在ANSSI ASCAD数据集上模拟关键排名达18±1.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设固定近距离探头，低估了空中 adversaries 的威胁。论文旨在提出一种能应对空中自主攻击的仿真框架。

Method: 设计四无人机编队架构：三架空间专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器加载泄露、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄露）+ 一架汇聚无人机。汇聚无人机执行相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和基于空间分离泄露流中心积分的二阶掩码抵消。采用双通道CNN进行汇聚处理，并通过互相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动。

Result: 在ANSSI ASCAD数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128及50/100样本去同步变体）上，模拟关键排名达18±1.7（0.25 m，五个种子）。交叉相关对齐将单无人机排名从89降至21（100样本抖动变体）。双通道CNN损失收敛至0.454（随机基线5.545），改善去同步数据集排名。

Conclusion: TriSweep仿真验证了多无人机编队进行远距离自主电磁侧信道攻击的可行性，下一步计划制造物理原型。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>
